# Seed 73 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - jitexec fairly weighed, memory without the applications, the true front (Iteration 544)

`seed 73 11h widths noevolved then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip
~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16` at 97ede934 (Iteration 521 - 520's code: 521 changed only results/). 32,876
designs, generations 0-128 (26,863 alive, 5,981 screened, 32 died "wrong output: apps" - every one cpt16); islands off;
2,989 designs an hour (seed 72: 2,916). Speed over the seven workloads, as seeds 71-72's - the three compare.

**The front, two ways** (532): the true front - dominates() over the run's records (review-checks.py votes --db;
results/drafts/seed73-front-544.py) - 222 designs; the results file's (the run's first_front, the shortcut the
after-seed-73 batch fixes) 174; 61 in both. Every share and vote below is the true front's; heldout, curse, appbench,
the gate and ans-check read the results file's.

**No verdict - no comparison in this chain** (rule 1). The spread at generation 102 (in-run speeds; session 60 of
results/compare-fronts-amd-ryzen-7-pro-8840hs.md): fastest 0.1469 against seed 72's 0.1485 and 71's 0.1452, smallest
14,079 against 14,079 and 14,210 - inside the small-run bars on the two that compare; gene usage 6.8-6.9 points from
71 and 72 (two runs of one start: up to 17 in a gene).

**The fixed-box hypervolume** (GOALS.md, "When the improvements are done"; lab/evolve/hv.py): **0.34915**, all 222
inside the box - the series' first value. The extremes: fastest fc680274ba 0.1399 (38,344 bytes), smallest 2cbc65f49a
14,055 bytes, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1772. The stopping count starts at seed 74 (the batch changes
the method).

**Memory without the applications** (the after-seed-72 batch): the true front's least 112 KB, its most 268 KB - the six
old workloads' range again, as the checklist expected (100-140 KB for the least); seeds 71-72 measured the
applications' data.

**jitexec, fairly weighed - prediction 518 fails**: on 6 of the true front's 65 JIT designs (9%) and 6 of the fastest
tenth's 22 (every one a JIT design) - up from 2-3 designs in seeds 70-72, short of a quarter and of most. jitfuse 7 and
7 (its votes count from this run); jitmul 45 and 22; jitcfa 2 and 1; jitret 4, jitvar 1; jitdoes, jitrp, jitos 0.

**The votes** (the true front): all four removal votes - d256, jitdoes, jitrp, jitret, jitvar; over 71-73 no freeze
candidate, jitrp 8 of 8 (its two counted runs). **The freeze question** (533): rtimm is not a candidate - seed 73 did not
give it four votes (rtimm on 1 front design, rtfuse on 5); rtfuse not before seed 75's processing.

**The rules** (review-checks.py rules): 2 jitret PASS (a keep vote); 3 ATTENTION - the fast end re-measured / in-run
1.070 (1.028-1.101), the 7% offset of open item 3 (seed 74's clock test); 4 PASS; 5 ATTENTION in name only - the 61
32-bit failures are every one a cpt16 design alive at 64 bits, "wrong output: apps": the 16-bit reach refused out loud
at 4-byte cells, as seed 72's 67 (517), a cause fail32 does not list yet; 6 PASS (14,055); 7 PASS (rtiplus on 18%,
working); 8 tail retired - its code removed, FROZEN at 0: its votes are the freeze's own; 9 PASS (1 design relies on the
overlap: no change); 10 the chain's run-tests.

Facts: held out (heldout.py, the 174), sieve's Spearman 0.961 (fastest tenth -0.304), regret +2.9%; appbench.py on 16
of the 174 - the fastest's geometric mean 0.289 of s6 (seed 72: 0.275), Spearman with the in-run speed 0.897 (0.797),
every output right; progress - the smallest gained in 21 generations (the last at 127 of 128; 7 gains, 72 bytes in the
last third), the best speed by 2% or more in 16 (the last at 109); the remote checks of seed 72's front
(results/xcheck-seed73.md): Spearman 0.870, 0.931, 0.893 at dream's three builds, 0.882 on the Tegra (seed 71's front:
0.955-0.958 and 0.916); the pack's own checks PASS, 7 IDENTICAL, jail PASS; results-measured-here.patch 4 files, not
applied.

---

# Evolved VM designs

26895 designs evaluated, 26863 alive, 5981 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): e31c9f1086, speed 0.386, binary + image 19,382, memory 124 KB - Q 0.274 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
